## Psalms 1:1 (#1) (id: en_tq_PSA_wog5)

## Psalms 150:6 (#1) (id: 178533)

**Who should praise Yahweh?**

Everything that breathes should praise Yahweh.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 150:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)
